२. लेखापरी क्षण

प्रस्ताबना —

सहकार आयुकत च निवंधक, सहकारी संस्था, महाराष्ट्र राज्य, पुणे पांचे
कार्यालयात अपर RSE, सहकारी संस्था, (लेग्डापरीक्षण) पहागाष्ट्र राज्य,
पुणे है फद असून त्यांचें अधिपत्याखाली सहनिबंधक सहकारी संस्था
[लिखापरिक्षण) महाराष्ट्र राज्य पुणें हे एक पद तसेच विशेष लेखापरिक्षक
चा. १ या दजाची दोन RIG अधिकाऱ्यांची पढें कार्यरत आहेत. WET
अधिकारी AEST आवडत यांना राज्याताल सहकारी संस्थाच्या लेखापरिक्षण
क॑ अनुष्का कायकाजाचे नियमन च नियंत्रण करण्यासाठी सहाय्य करतात
राज्यामध्ये मुंबई, पुणे, नाशिक, कोल्हापूर, उत्पती संभानीनगर, अमरावती
नागपूर या सात ay बिंभागोप सहनिबंधक (लेखापरीक्षण) हे विभाग
स्वरावर ASS करतात तसेच जिल्हा स्तरावर जिल्हा fee लेधापरिसक
apt.) San संरंधा हैं कोपकाजं ARTA, त्यांच्या मदतीस अन्य सिशेष
PITT यगे-र व २ तसेच क्षत्रप स्तरावर त्यांचे अधिनस्त कममचारी
बग पुरविण्यात आलेला. आहे. याशिवाय कांही शिखिर संस्थांच्या
लेखापरिक्षणासादी STARTS RATT पदे कार्यरत शत. (उदा
पणन महासंष,पुविकास उक्त, गहनिमाण इ.)

महाराष्ट्र सहकारों संस्था अधिनियम ९९६० 'चे कलम दर आणि महाराष्ट्र
सहकारी aT नियम (९६२१ मध्ये लेखापीक्षणाची संकल्पना विद केलेली
आहे. कलग ८१ अन्यपे सर्व सहकारी RATA त्यांचे वैधानिक लेखापरीक्षण
यापिक AAT सभेपूर्वी पृ करण्याची जबाबदारी संस्थांवर सोपवलेली
आहे. AS प्रत्येक दोन बर्षानितर लोेगागरिक्षकांची नाप्तालिका या
कायालयाच्या स्तरावरून तयार कहव शासन मात्यतेने प्रसिध्द केली जाते,
या नामतालिकेत सनदी लेखापाल. सनदी लेखापालाची भागीदारी फर्म, परिव्यय
लैखापात, परिव्यय लेखापरिक्षकांची भागीदारी फर्म, प्रमाणित लेखापरीक्षक
व सहकार छात्यातोल लेबापरोक्षंकांचा सपाधेश असून निय्प ६९ पथ्ये
नयु केलेल्या ले्डापनिक्षेकांच्या व संस्थांच्या वर्गवारीनुयार पाव लेखागरिक्षकंची
संबंधीत संस्थेने बापिक सर्वसाधारण सपत नियुक्‍तो करून संस्थेचे लेखापरिक्षण
पु करुन घ्याचचांचें आहे; संस्थन Slee कलम ok Tah
प्रपाणें लेखागरिझकाची नेपणुक कफन कलम ७९(रव) नुसारे स्याचप्रभाणे
ae निर्षधकाम सादर न केल्यास, अधिनियपातील ee ८६1)
ae Tad निषंषक नामताललिकेवरील पाज PRR संबंधीत
संख्येचे सेखापरिक्षण करन ET अशी तातुद आहे. संबंधात लेखापागिक्षकाने
Se तरतुदोनुसार संस्थेचे AT आधिक बर्ष संपल्यापासून
४ महिन्याचे आत पूर्ण करुन लेखापरीक्षण अहवाल लाडापरिक्षण पुणे
केल्याच्या दिलांवापासून एक महित्याच्या आत व कोणत्याही परिस्थितीत
वार्षिक सदसाधारण सभच्या नाटोंस निगमनाचे दिनांवरपूर्वी निवंधकास व
ery सादर करावयाचा आहे.

५७ व्या SANT पुळे अ्षधिनिषव झालेल्या सुधारणेस अनुसरन
लेजापरिकण विभागातील अधिकारों /कर्मचारों ores लीत कापे पार
ET गातात

१, महाराष्ट्र सहकरी संस्था अधिनियमातोल कलम ८ शु१४अ। अन्ये
संस्था उरायाद्दार उपलब्ध होणारे सहकारी संस्थाचे लेखापरीक्षण कापकाज
थे निमंष्रक कार्यालयाकडील परंतुकाखालील आदेशाने उपलब्ध होणारे
सहकारी संख्यांचे लेखापरीक्षण कापकाज करें.

9. महाराष्ट्र सफारी संस्वा अधिनियम (९६० थे कायदा झालम
८((8॥/बो नुसार सहकारी संस्थाचे Wes Te तपासणी कामकाज व
कायदा कलग 4 ay) अन्वयेचे सहकारी संस्थाचे चाचणी लेण्ापरीक्षण
कामकाज तसेच कायदा कलम ct (६) अन्वये सहकारी संस्थांचे
NT AT काणे,

3, महाराष्ट्र सहक्तागी संस्था अधिनिवमातील कलम १०८ अन्वये
मंवसापनातील ARR संस्थांचे लेखापरिक्षण कामकान कराणें

४, महाराष्ट्र सहकारी संख्या आंधनियपातील कलम Athy) अन्वये
TRI से संख्यांचे SRAM विहोत TAT पुर्ण काण्याचे
नुपंगाने लेखापरिक्षण न झालेल्या / अपुर्ण असलेल्या संस्थांचा बेळोवेळो,
जिल्हा, OM व TERT आढाचा घेऊन राज्यातील सर्वे संस्थांचे
लेखापरिक्षण पूण होण्याच्या अनुपंगान नियपन च Per कामकाज
करा.

७. महाराष्ट्र सहकारी संस्था अधिनियम (९६० चे कलम ८३ वे ८८
Saat सहकारी ARTS प्राधीकृत चोकशी अधिकारों म्हणून A
कारणों,

Marathwada.

farmers residing outside the area of operation as
mentioned in the licence of the Money Lenders were
not eligible for the benefit under the earlier scheme,
Accordingly the said condition inthe government decisian
dated 10.04.2015 has been relaxed once as per the
government decisian dated 13.09.20) 9,

the amount of Rs.9,00,64,525/- received trom the
government for loan warver given by 364 money lenders
in 14 distnets of Vidarbha and Marathwada te a total of
3205 borrower farmers outside the area mentioned in

Rs.38,57,410/- has been surmended. Therefore loan

5. Co-operative Housing Societies for Government emplovees. Introduction:

For giving financial assistance to the state government
emplovees for construction of ther own houses under
the scheme of loan to Housing Societies for the State
Government emplovees and similarly as a remedy for
the difficulties faced regarding construction of houses
in the rural and urban areas, the government has started
this scheme from 06/12/1907, From 13/09/1971 the
same echeme is mule applicable to the Distnet Counce
employees tow as to the State Government employees.
From 6° December, 1967 to 24/01/1980 this scheme
was implemented by the Finance department. As this
scheme is bemge tmplemented through the medium of
Co-operative Sector, from 24th January, 1980 the same
8 transfered to the then Agriculture and Co-operation
department. At present the said scheme is being
implemented through the Co-operation section in. the
Co-operation, Marketing and Textile Industry department.

FPeecoligritics of the Scheme :—

The monthly subscriptions being deducted from the
solanes of State Government ond District Council
emplovees under Group Insurance scheme. that fund
was deposited in the Finance department in lumpsum
ond from that fond the finance department makes
provisions lor this scheme to the Co-operation
department as per budget and this find ४ utilized through
the Co-operutive Housing Soctetres for construction of
houses oftheir ownership night for the Siate government
and District council emplovees. For this purpose the
povernment need nol sanction separate find, The
amounts in this fund are sanctioned to the employees
in the form of loan. on that the government receives
revenue in the form of interest, Since the reeovery of
loan is made directly from the monthly salaries of the
employees, the quantum of loan recovery is. nearly
100%. The capital and interest are recovered oll the
time the debtor member is in the Government service.
Over and above this 11 unvthing is remuming, the same
will be recovered from the retirement benefits of the
debtor member. Means the quantum ol loan recovery
5 100%. The rte of interest the Finance department
fixes for Housing advance, the same mite will be
applicable to this scheme

Other issucs of the Scheme :—

a. The members of the Co-operative Housing
Societies of the government ond District Councils
registered under Maharashtra Co-operative Societies
Act, 1960, will be sanctioned amount of 50 multiples of
basic salary + admissible dearness allowance of
maximinm Rs. 15.00,000/- or cost of the house of

Dhananjavrac Gadgil Institute of Co-operative
Tmunagement, Nagpur :-

Dhananjayrao Gadgil Institute of Co-operative
minagement, Nagpur working under National Council
for Co-operative Training. New Delhiis established on

National Council for Co-operative Training, Central
Government and State Goverment in the ratie of 50:50,
This institute provides short term and long term need
based tramme to the Co-operative department official,
employees, afftcial-directors and members of Co-
operative Societies. This institute along with Co-
operative traning provides guidance and advice over
vartous topics. The Commissioner of Co-operation 1s the
chairman of Annual Programme Advisory Committee

of this instutute. Under his chammanslup Annual Traming
Programme of this institute ts formulated. Inthe financial
year 2024-2025 this institute has organized 217 training
programmes and trained | | 086 officers, employees and
members and added valuable contribution m professional
Hunwn Resource Development which ts needed for Co-
operative movement of Maharashtra.

G. D.C, & A, EXAMINATION

The G, D.C_&.4. Board which has been constituted im 1936 under the Chairmanship of the Commissioner for
Co-operation and Registrar, 5. M.S. Pune is working since then as per the approved rules of the Board. The
Rules have been changed from time to time and the examination was being conducted annually at 9 centres as per
the Rules laid down in Govt. Resolution No, CSG 1580/2560) V1 CR-287/12-C dated [3/1/1981 issued by
the Agriculture and Co-aperation Department. Since May, 1990 the examination was conducted annually at 26
District centres. but From May, 1995 ihe examination is conducted at those centers as per the administrative
decision taken from time to time. Al present examination is conducted on 16 centres,

The purpose of this examination isto make an endeavor to develop awareness about the Co-operative movement
asa Whole amongst workers employed in various sprees of Co-operative field, [tis also.a qualifying examination
for the promotion to Head clerk, Co-operative officer (grade-IL) and Auditor (कपट) from lower cadre. [tis a
compulsory to pass the examination to those who are directly recruited as Co-operative officer grade-I & 11,
Auiitor, grade | & [Land who are directly recruit as.a class A & B gazetted officer in the department. The
employees of various Co-operative societies who are under graduate and have completed continuous five years
service in the institution are allowed to.appear for this examination, similarly, those applicants who are graduate of
any faculty जाएं also allowed to uppear for this examination.

८. महाराष्ट्र राज्य सहकारी परिषद, पूणे

महाराट्र सहकारी संस्था अधिनियम ९६६० चें कलम १५४-अ (१) EM महाराष्ट्र राज्य सहकारी परिषदे या नादाने संबोधण्यात येणारी
एक परिषद अमल आणि ती अप्यक्ष आणि उपाध्यक्ष यांसह जितके सदस्य राज्य शासन खेळोंखेळी निशिवत व नामनि्देशित करीत तितके सदस्य मिळून
बनलेला असेल. (२) राज्य ज्ञासन परिषदेच्या सचिवाची नेमणूक फ़रील.

Maharashtra State Co-Operative Council, Pune
Section 154-A Constitution of State Cooperative Council, There shall be a council to be called the
Maharashtra State Cooperatrve Council consisting of such numberof members including the Chairman and Vice
Chariman as State Govimay detenmine,
The State Govtshall appoint a seeretary of the council, The State Govt, appointed Shin Rayan Baburao

Patil, former M.L.A. Solapur as a Chairman of the Maharashtra State Cooperative Council Pune vide Govern-
ment Decision No. Sankirna-2024/P.Nol02/22-8, dated 27th Seplember 2024, Government of Maharashtra as

per the provision of Sec. | 54-A( 1) ofthe Maharashtra Cooperative Society Act, 1960

The functions of Council constituted under sub-section (1) shall be as follows -

(1) Toadvise the State Government on all matters relating to Cooperative movement.

(2) Toreview the Co-operative movement and to suggest ways of co-oradinating the activities of co-operative
socienes in the State.

(3) To suggest ways and means to remove the difficulties expenenced by the co-operative societies in
the State.

(4) To report to the State Government on such matters as may be referred to it by the State Government,

(5) Torecommend the plans and policies for the development of cooperative movement in the State.

(6) To evaluate existing schemes and suppest new schemes for Cooperative development especially for the
developement of backward classes and economacally weaker sections of the sactety.

(7) To advise the State Government for the implementation of special scheme of economic development through
Co-operative methods and

(8) Toundertake studies for any of the purposes aforesaid either through department or specialised bodies. For
the year 2026-27 there 15 budgetary provision of Rs. 10.00 lakh for this State Co-operative Couneil.

Commissionerate of Sugar Maharashtra State Pune

Reconstruction of Sugar Commissionerate :-

In order have to quantitive and qualitative growth of
sugar industry and to solve the problems faced by
industry with respect lomvestment, rehabilitation of
sick mills and to look aller complimentry business and
to have proper planning and monitorme of sugar
industry, government has established independant
Directorate of Sugar in 1971.

The Directorate of Sugar has been upgraded as
“Sugar Commissionerte” inthe year 1991.

Details of posts working under Sugar
Commisslonerite न

As per sanctioned Staffing pattern of sugar
commissionerate, their are total 102 post on the
establishment. The posts are Commussioner of sugar -
| post. Director of Sugar (2 post, Joint Director
(Sugar) 04 post, Executive Engineer-Ol past, Jonnt
Registrar (Audit) - 01 Post, Statastycian-()! post, Asstt
Director (Sugar) -08 post, Asst.Director (Agni) -()1
post, Account Officer जी | Post, Class C-67 and Class
D 13 post.

Structure of Sugar Commissionerate :-

Under Sugar Commuissionerate there are हि regional
offices working at Pune, Kolhapur, Solapur,
Ahilyanagar, Chatrapati Sambhajmagar, Nanded,
Amaravii and Nagpur Regional उगा Director (RJD)
of Sugar is head of regional office. There are Regional
Deputy Director to assist him, Apart from this, there
is agricultural officer of Class B cadre working in the
RJD office from agriculture department. There are
Class-C and Class-D employees working in the office
of RID. Similarly 35 Postof Special Auditor, Class-A
dealing with audit of the Sugar Factones working under
Regional Joint Director (Sugar)

The dominance of sugar industry in the rural area has
made revelution in the field of social, economical,
educational and health. As on today there are 200
co-operative sugar factories. Maharashtra State
contnibute 27 "> of the total sugar production of the
country. The state was leading sugar producer state
since 1974-75 amoung the various States of the India,

(one) and (two) ofthe goverment agricultural produce

been amended as per the amended provision voting
rights have been provided to the members of the
managing committee of Agricultural Credit Societies

Election Authority, pursuant to the amendment made
under the Maharashtra Agricultural Produce
Marketing/ Development and Regulation) (Second
amendment) act, 2020, the authority vide order
dt.29.4.2021. The District Deputy Registrar of Co-
openitive sociely has been declared as District Election
officer (A.P.MLC), of that district. For the purpose of
enlection of all Agricultural Produce Marketing

2. Agri. Export Policy (AEP) of Maharashtra
State :

the states agricultural export pollicy and submitted itto
the State Government,

Objectives +

2) Entrepreneurship development mAgn, Export,

trachitienal and non-toxitional agro products exports
from Maharashtra.

5, Creatron of efficient export onented infrastructure
and wiihization of existing infrastructure.

6. To provide an institutional mechanism te undertake
export-oriented activities.

T. Skill development of the concermed stakeholders in
the agriexport.

AGRICULTURAL PLEDGE LOAN SCHEME

In harvesting season there ts huge amival of particular
commedity in the market in very short pero, which
causes substantial fall inmarket pnces of the commodity
Farmers do not hove capacity to hokl them stock so they

value as loon, As the prices of the commodity rise in the
market the farmer sells his produce in the market and
repaivé his loan and thus fetches higher price for his
produce at reasonable levels.

Government of Maharathira is implementing Asian
Development Bank assisted Maharashtra Agnbusiness
Network (MAGNET) Project. 14 horticulture crops (he
Pomegranate, Banana, Guava, Sapota, Orange, Sweet

objectives of the project are to increase farmers income
while promoting commercialy altractive private
investment in selected 15 horticultural value chains,
reduce post-harvest losses and extension of the
horticulture shelf-life, crate demand-linked value addition
and increase participation of farmers producer
organizations (FPOs) in value chains. Project is being
miplemented m the entire state.

Project omplementation period 8६ 6 years, There are
three main outputs ofthe project under output | capacity
building of farmer producer organizations working in the
selected 14 horticulture crops and floneluliune ts being
done 10 increase productivity and quality of produce, post
harvest handling, to meet food security and quality
requirement of expert and domestic markets etc. Under

and term: loan to FPOs: and VCOs through partner

Cotton Season 2075-2026:-

The Central Government has announced the
fuaranteed prices of cotton for the cotton season 2025-
2026, The guaranteed price of medium staple cotton
and long staple cotton has been increased by Rs. 58)
per quintal each compared to the previous cothon season.
The guaranteed price of medium staple cotton is
Rs. 77107. per quintal while the auarunteed price of long
staple cotton 15:95; #110/-per quintal.

Cotten prices depend on the country’s total ection
producton, crop conditions, domestic entten consumption,
import-export, and colton prices in the intemational
market. To prevent losses to cotton farmers if the price
of cotton in the open market falls below the minimum

105

guaraniced price, the cotton ts purchased at the minimum
guaranteed price announced by the central government,

In the state, cotton 15 procured by Cotton Comporation
of India (CCH), the Nodal Agent of Government of India
and by Maharashtra State Cooperatrve Cotton Producers
Marketing Federation, as a Sub Agent of CCI announced
by the Central Government ot the minemum guaranteed
price.

The Cotten Corporation of India has opened a total
of 168 cotton procurement centers in the state, 79 in
Chhatrapatt Sambhajinagar division and 89 in Akola
division, for the cotton procurement in the 2075-2026
cotton season:

As on Dated 08.01 2026, 91,445,453 quintals of cotton
have been purchased from farmers at the minimum
support price through the Cotten Corporation of India.

Fruit & Vegetable Co-operative Societies

Ont of the total land, about M0011 hectors of land in
Maharashtra Siate are under horticultural crops such 15
Mungo, Grapes, Bananas, pomegranates, chikous etc

These Secities are registered wilh the objective of
producing fruits and vegetables on a large seule
following which the agricultural produce undergoes many
stages such as storing. Grading. Pre-cooling,
transportation through vehicles, so that itis fit for export
to Foreign countries, also so that the farmer gets a fair
price for his produce and the customer-also gets the
fruits and vegetables ata reasonable price,

Aims objectives of frait and vegatable societies ६८

|) Grading, Stocking, Packing and ale of fruit and
vegetable and other perishable produce.

2) Supplying fertilizers.

3) Sale of seeds, pesticides, and other farming
related tems.

4) Proper marketing of agricultural produce so as to
ive a fair price to the farmers, besides other agricultural
related activities,

The विणा und vegetable societies connot carry oul
any business relating to fertilizers lor a period of three
years from date of Registration. However they can
indulge in saleo! organic fertilizers,

These socicties can alse provide services like tissue
culture, pre-cooling and similar services. With the
intention of export of certain agricultural produce such
as Mangoes, Grapes, Bananas, Oranges ete. under
section 2041) of Maharashtra Co-operative Societies
Act 190, some partnership firms such us Maha Mango,
Miho Grapes, Maha Banana, Maha Orange were
registered. All these societies ure working towards
getting more and more foreign exchange through export
of their produces,

DIRECTORATE OF SERICULTURE

Introduction :-

The stote of Maharashtra ts a Non-Traditional silk
producing state. Maharashtra ranks first in silk production
among Non-Traditional states and eighth among silk
producing states m the country. The “senculture Industry”
is based on agriculture and has a huge employment
potential for people in rural areas. This industry 15 able
torsion the migration of people fron rural areas 10 cities.
Tis the only assured silk industry providing employment
epportunities to peeple of all classes and age proups of
the the society, 1 15 an income sustaining agri-indusiry
and has the potential 10 end the financial distress of
farmers. The silk mdustry has the potential to chage the
face of rural areas. The special features of the serculiure
industry are high monthly income with low capital, rural
employment potential and access te a vibrant market.

Considering the nmportance of the silk industry, the
Government of Maharashtra has established a separate
Directorate for sericulture under the administrative
contro! of Department of Textile On September 1, 1997
withats hendgquarters at Nagpur. Serculture development
works are camed out through 27 Dystriet Sericulture
OWNees in the State, which are controlled by Divisional
Offices at Nagpur, वा, Chhatrapan: Sambhajimagur
and Pure,

Scope Of Sericulture In Mahwrashtra State:-

Both Mulberry Silk and Tasar Silk are produced im
the state of Maharashtra. Tribals from Gadehireli.
Chandrapur, Bhandora and Gondia districts have been
producing lasar Cocoons in the forest for 250 yeurs.
Recently Tasar sericulture ts introduced on experimental
basis in Thane, Yovatmal Districts of the state, Alsa,
Mulberry und Taser sericulture is practiced 27 districts
01 the state

In the year 2024-25, a total of [9405 farmers in the
state have 20913.50 acres of land under Mulberry
cultivation and 3409.00 MT of Cocoon was produced.
Out of which $32.00 MT. Mulberry Silk yur has been
produced and it has created 91.32 lakh man days of
employment, Alsa, in Tasar Silk Industry, [330
beneticines used 3954 hectares of Ain and Arjuna food
trees to supply the DFL's 5.95 Lakh to prodwee 191,59
lakh cocoons und 10.441 MT of Raw Silk has been
produced whieh has created 15,66 lakh man-days of
employment.
